[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m 首先，根据切线的性质，∠AOS 和 ∠BQO 都是直角三角形的两个锐角。根据圆内接四边形的性质，∠AOS + ∠BQO = 180° - ∠APB。因为∠APB=40°，所以 ∠AOS + ∠BQO = 140°。由于∠AOS 和 ∠BQO 都是直角，我们可以得出 ∠AOS = ∠BQO = 70° / 2 = 35°。
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m 现在我们可以计算AP=AB-BP=2√2-√2=√2。
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322863)[0m

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m The problem involves a tangent at point B and a circle's geometry. The tangent theorem states that the tangent to a circle from an external point is equal in length to the radius to the point of tangency. Here, AB is the tangent and BP is the radius extending to point B from the center P. The triangles formed are right triangles.
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m Since AB is the tangent and PB is the radius, BP is perpendicular to AB. This makes triangle PAB a right triangle. We can use the Pythagorean theorem to find the length of PA, which is the hypotenuse AB in this case.
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m AB = 10 (given)
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PB = 6 (radius of the circle)
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m Using the Pythagorean theorem:
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA^2 = PB^2 + AB^2
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA^2 = 6^2 + 10^2
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA^2 = 36 + 100
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA^2 = 136
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA = √136
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA = √(4 * 34)
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m PA = 2√34
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m Now, since PA is a tangent segment and MB is also a tangent segment from the same external point M, they are equal. Therefore, MB = y.
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m MB = PA
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m y = 2√34
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m Finally, we need to find the numerical value of y:
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m y ≈ 2 * √34
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m y ≈ 2 * 5.831
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m y ≈ 11.662
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m Rounded to the nearest tenth:
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m y ≈ 11.7

[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    where \( \theta \) is the central angle in degrees.
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the sector} = \frac{130^\circ}{360^\circ} \times 9\pi = \frac{130}{360} \times 9\pi = \frac{13}{36} \times 9\pi = \frac{13}{4} \pi
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 3. **Calculate the area of the shaded region:**
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the shaded region} = \text{Area of the circle} - \text{Area of the sector}
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the shaded region} = 9\pi - \frac{13}{4} \pi
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the shaded region} = \pi \left(9 - \frac{13}{4}\right)
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the shaded region} = \pi \left(\frac{36}{4} - \frac{13}{4}\right) = \pi \left(\frac{23}{4}\right) = \frac{23}{4} \pi
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 4. **Convert the result to a decimal and round to the nearest tenth:**
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \frac{23}{4} \pi \approx 5.75 \pi
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    5.75 \pi \approx 5.75 \times 3.14159 \approx 18.05
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m 
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    Rounding to the nearest tenth:
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \[
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \text{Area of the shaded region} \approx 18.1
[36m(WorkerDict pid=3322200)[0m    \]
